     MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
     管理層討論與分析

     Business Review                                                 業務回顧
     The development of healthcare sector in China has become        隨著我國新一輪醫療改革十年來的分段推進
     unprecedentedly important following China’s latest stage of     和有序落實，以及「健康中國」戰略的提出，
     medical reform having been efficiently launched for ten         醫療健康事業在我國的重要性提升到了前所
     years and with the government’s implementation of the           未有的高度。人口老齡化、疾病譜變化，季
     “Healthy China” strategy. The aging population, changes in      節性傳染病及人民對於健康生活的追求等因
     the disease spectrum, seasonal infectious diseases and          素使大眾對於醫療服務的需求呈持續上升
     people’s pursuit of a healthy life are driving the demand for   趨勢。
     medical services.

     In late 2019, the Standing Committee of the National            2019年年底，全國人大常委會審議通過《基
     People’s Congress approved and issued the “Basic Medical        本醫療衛生與健康促進法》，內容涵蓋衛生
     and Health Promotion Law” covering different aspects of the     健康領域的各個方面，在總結醫藥衛生體制
     field of healthcare. Based on the experience of national        改革經驗的基礎上，對我國衛生健康工作作
     reform of the medical system, it has based on the principle     出了頂層的、網絡性的安排，凸顯「保基本、
     of “maintaining basic coverage, enhancing capabilities of       強基層、促健康」理念，更以立法形式保障社
     lower-level medical institutions and facilitating the system    會資本辦醫療機構公平享受各項權利。報告
     and mechanism” to make a top-to-bottom and systemic             期內，國家衛生健康委員會聯同國家發展和
     arrangement, and ensure various rights of medical               改革委員會、財政部、國家醫療保障局等十
     institutions operated by social capital under the new law.      個部委共同發佈了《關於促進社會辦醫持續
     During the Reporting Period, the National Health                健康規範發展的意見》，堅持政府主導與發
     Commission, together with ten government authorities            揮市場機製作用相結合，提出加大政府支持
     including the National Development and Reform                   社會辦醫力度、簡化準入行業的審批服務、
     Commission, the Ministry of Finance and the National            明確公立醫療機構與社會辦醫分工合作等舉
     Healthcare Security Administration, jointly issued the          措，進一步促進社會資本辦醫療機構的持續
     “Opinions on Promoting the Sustainable and Regulated            健康規範發展。社會辦醫作為我國醫療衛生
     Development of Social Entities’ Participation in Healthcare     服務體系的重要組成部分，將在政策範圍內
     Industry” to encourage the development of medical               迎來更充分的發展空間。同時，取消藥品和
     institutions by social capital with the government support,     耗 材 加 成、實 施 國 家 層 面 藥 品 帶 量 採 購 政
     simplifying the procedures in seeking government approval       策、改革醫保支付方式等一系列醫改措施，
     for industry’s entry, clarifying the complementary              在為調整醫療服務收費價格騰出了空間的同
     mechanism between public and private medical institutions,      時，也將考驗醫院的精細化管理能力。
     and further promoting the sustainable and healthy
     development of private medical institutions. Medical
     institutions as established by social capital shall play an
     important role in China’s medical and healthcare service
     system, and have great potential in business development.
     At the same time, a series of measures of the medical
     reform such as the elimination of mark-ups on drugs and
     consumables, the implementation of national-level
     Centralised Drugs Procurement Policy, and the reform of
     medical insurance payment methods have created rooms for
     price adjustment of medical services, and increased the
     incentive for hospitals to further refine its operation and
     management.

     dividends received from the Company’s subsidiaries. The        受限於開曼群島法律與本公司組織章程細則
     payment of the dividend by the Company is also subject to      之任何限制所規限。

     YAN HUA IOT AGREEMENT DISPUTE                                 燕化IOT協議爭議
     On January 21, 2019, the Company received a letter from 於2019年1月21日，本公司收到燕化鳳凰及
     Yan Hua Phoenix and Yan Hua Hospital to unilaterally 燕化醫院之信函通知其自2019年1月21日起
     terminate the Yan Hua IOT Agreement with effect from 單方面終止燕化IOT協議。本集團已於2019
     January 21, 2019. On April 17, 2019, the Group has 年4月17日就燕化IOT協議爭議向北京市第二
     submitted the civil claim statement against Yan Hua Phoenix 中級人民法院遞交民事起訴狀起訴燕化鳳凰
     and Yan Hua Hospital in relation to the Yan Hua IOT 及燕化醫院，請求法院判定燕化鳳凰及燕化
     Agreement dispute to Beijing Second Intermediate People’s 醫 院2019年1月21日 單 方 面 解 除 燕 化IOT協
     Court on the even date to seek the court’s ruling that the 議的行為無效，以及燕化鳳凰及燕化醫院須
     unilateral termination of Yan Hua IOT Agreement by Yan 支付其因違反燕化IOT協議所造成的違約損
     Hua Phoenix and Yan Hua Hospital on January 21, 2019 失。於2019年12月18日，北 京 市 第 二 中 級
     shall be void and that Yan Hua Phoenix and Yan Hua 人 民 法 院 作 出（民 事 判 決 書[2019]京02民 初
     Hospital should be liable for damages for breach of the Yan 第304號）民事判決，主要內容如下：(1)宣告

Auditor’s responsibilities for the audit of                      核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔
the consolidated financial statements                            的責任
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about          我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不
whether the consolidated financial statements as a whole         存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述
are free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud        取得合理保證，並出具包括我們意見的核數
or error, and to issue an auditor’s report that includes our     師報告。我們僅對全體股東作出報告，除此
opinion. Our report is made solely to you, as a body, and for    以外，本報告並無其他用途。我們不會就核
no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards        數師報告的內容向任何其他人士負上或承擔
or accept liability to any other person for the contents of      任何責任。
this report.

Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not 合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按照
a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with 《香港審計準則》進行的審計，在某一重大錯
HKSAs will always detect a material misstatement when it 誤陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺
exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are 詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或匯
considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they 總起來可能影響綜合財務報表使用者依賴綜
could reasonably be expected to influence the economic 合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，則有關的錯
decisions of users taken on the basis of these consolidated 誤陳述可被視作重大。
financial statements.

As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise        在根據《香港審計準則》進行審計的過程中，

         New definition of a lease                                  租賃的新定義
         Under IFRS 16, a contract is, or contains, a lease         根據國際財務報告準則第16號，
         if the contract conveys a right to control the use         若合約表明在一段時間內為換取
         of an identified asset for a period of time in             代價而擁有控制一項已識別資產
         exchange for consideration. Control is conveyed            的使用權，則該合約為租賃或包
         where the customer has both the right to obtain            含租賃。倘客戶有權從使用已識
         substantially all of the economic benefits from            別資產中獲取絕大部分經濟利益
         use of the identified asset and the right to direct        及有權主導已識別資產的使用，
         the use of the identified asset. The Group                 則表示擁有控制權。本集團選擇
         elected to use the transition practical expedient          使用過渡可行權宜方法，僅在首
         allowing the standard to be applied only to                次應用日期對先前應用國際會計
         contracts that were previously identified as leases        準則第17號及國際財務報告詮釋
         applying IAS 17 and IFRIC 4 at the date of initial         委員會第4號識別為租賃的合約應
         application. Contracts that were not identified as         用該準則。根據國際會計準則第
         leases under IAS 17 and IFRIC 4 were not                   17號及國際財務報告詮釋委員會
         reassessed. Therefore, the definition of a lease           第4號並無識別為租賃的合約不予
         under IFRS 16 has been applied only to contracts           重估。因此，國際財務報告準則

   substantive process that together significantly                程序。業務毋須擁有所有產生輸出物所
   contribute to the ability to create output. A business         需的輸入項目及程序而存在。有關修訂

   acquired inputs and acquired substantive processes             注於向客戶提供的貨物或服務、投資收

   outputs. The amendments have also narrowed the                 有關修訂就所取得的程序是否實質作出

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT                                    2.4 主要會計政策概要
    ACCOUNTING POLICIES
  Investments in associates and joint                           對聯營公司及合營公司之投資
  ventures
  An associate is an entity in which the Group has a            聯營公司為本集團於其一般不少於20%
  long term interest of generally not less than 20% of          股本投票權中擁有長期權益的實體，且
  the equity voting rights and over which it is in a            可對其發揮重大影響力。重大影響力指
  position to exercise significant influence. Significant       的是參與投資對象的財務和經營決策的
  influence is the power to participate in the financial        權力，但不是控制或共同控制這些決策
  and operating policy decisions of the investee, but is        的權力。
  not control or joint control over those policies.

  A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby        合營公司指一類共同安排，對安排擁有
  the parties that have joint control of the arrangement        共同控制權之訂約方據此有權享有共同
  have rights to the net assets of the joint venture. Joint     安排之資產淨值。共同控制指對某項安
  control is the contractually agreed sharing of control of     排之訂約協定共同控制，共同控制僅於
  an arrangement, which exists only when decisions              相關活動之決定需共享控制權之各方一
  about the relevant activities require the unanimous           致同意之時存在。
  consent of the parties sharing control.

  The Group’s investments in associates and a joint             本集團於聯營公司及合營公司的投資乃
  venture are stated in the consolidated statement of           按本集團根據權益會計法應佔資產淨值
  financial position at the Group’s share of net assets         減任何減值虧損於綜合財務狀況報表列
  under the equity method of accounting, less any               賬。倘會計政策存在任何不一致，將會
  impairment losses. Adjustments are made to bring into         作出相應調整。本集團應佔聯營公司或
  line any dissimilar accounting policies that may exist.       合營公司收購後業績及其他全面收益分
  The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results and         別計入綜合損益表及其他綜合全面收益
  other comprehensive income of associates and a joint          表。此外，倘於聯營公司或合營公司的
  venture is included in the consolidated statement of          權益直接確認變動，則本集團會於綜合
  profit or loss and consolidated other comprehensive           權益變動報表確認其應佔任何變動（倘
  income, respectively. In addition, when there has been        適用）。本集團與其聯營公司或合營公
  a change recognised directly in the equity of the             司間交易的未變現收益及虧損將以本集
  associate or joint venture, the Group recognises its          團於聯營公司或合營公司的投資為限對
  share of any changes, when applicable, in the                 銷，惟倘未變現虧損證明所轉讓資產減
  consolidated statement of changes in equity.                  值則除外。收購聯營公司所產生的商譽
  Unrealised gains and losses resulting from transactions       已計入作本集團於聯營公司或合營公司
  between the Group and its associates or joint venture         投資的一部份。
  are eliminated to the extent of the Group’s
  investments in the associates or joint venture, except
  where unrealised losses provide evidence of an
  impairment of the assets transferred. Goodwill arising
  from the acquisition of associates or joint ventures is
  included as part of the Group’s investments in
  associates or joint ventures.

           combination, the Group elects whether to measure the             計量屬現時擁有人權益並賦予擁有人權

           a proportionate share of net assets in the event of              其他部分乃按公允價值計量。收購相關

           the acquiree’s identifiable net assets. All other
           components of non-controlling interests are measured

           incurred.

           includes the separation of embedded derivatives in

           Fair value measurement                                           公允價值計量
           The Group measures its financial assets at FVTPL at              本集團於各呈報期末按公允價值計量以
           fair value at the end of each reporting period. Fair             公允價值計算且其變動計入損益的金融
           value is the price that would be received to sell an             資產。公允價值乃在市場參與者於計量
           asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly              日期進行的有序交易中出售資產所收取
           transaction between market participants at the                   或轉移負債所支付的價格。公允價值計
           measurement date. The fair value measurement is                  量乃基於假設出售資產或轉移負債的交
           based on the presumption that the transaction to sell            易於資產或負債的主要市場進行或於未
           the asset or transfer the liability takes place either in        有主要市場的情況下，則於資產或負債
           the principal market for the asset or liability, or in the       的最有利市場進行。主要或最有利市場
           absence of a principal market, in the most                       須位於本集團能到達的地方。資產或負
           advantageous market for the asset or liability. The              債的公允價值乃基於市場參與者為資產
           principal or the most advantageous market must be                或負債定價所用的假設計量（假設市場
           accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or           參與者依照彼等的最佳經濟利益行事）
                                                                                            。
           a liability is measured using the assumptions that
           market participants would use when pricing the asset
           or liability, assuming that market participants act in
           their economic best interest.

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT                                   2.4 主要會計政策概要（續）

   Fair value measurement (continued)                          公允價值計量（續）
   The Group uses valuation techniques that are                本集團使用適用於不同情況的估值方
   appropriate in the circumstances and for which              法，而其有足夠資料計量公允價值，以
   sufficient data are available to measure fair value,        盡量使用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減
   maximising the use of relevant observable inputs and        少使用不可觀察輸入數據。
   minimising the use of unobservable inputs.

   All assets and liabilities for which fair value is          公允價值於財務報表計量或披露的所有
   measured or disclosed in the financial statements are       資產及負債基於對公允價值計量整體屬
   categorised within the fair value hierarchy, described      重要的最低水平輸入數據按如下所述在
   as follows, based on the lowest level input that is         公允價值層級中分類：
   significant to the fair value measurement as a whole:

   Level 1 — based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in            第一級   — 根據可識別資產或負債於
             active markets for identical assets or                   活躍市場中所報未調整價
             liabilities                                              格

   Level 2 — based on valuation techniques for which           第二級   — 根據對公允價值計量具有
             the lowest level input that is significant to            重大影響的最低水平輸入
             the fair value measurement is observable,                數據可直接或間接被觀察
             either directly or indirectly                            的估值方法

   Level 3 — based on valuation techniques for which           第三級   — 根據對公允價值計量有重
             the lowest level input that is significant to            大影響的最低水平輸入數
             the fair value measurement is                            據不可觀察的估值方法
             unobservable

   For assets and liabilities that are recognised in the       對於在財務報表按經常性基準確認的資
   financial statements on a recurring basis, the Group        產及負債，本集團於各呈報期末根據對
   determines whether transfers have occurred between          公允價值計量整體屬重要的最低水平輸
   levels in the hierarchy by reassessing categorisation       入數據通過重估分類以確定各層級之間
   (based on the lowest level input that is significant to     是否出現轉移。
   the fair value measurement as a whole) at the end of
   each reporting period.

           through arrangement, it evaluates if, and to what                於何等程度保留資產所有權的風險及回

           transferred nor retained substantially all the risks and         權，則該資產將按本集團持續參與有關
           rewards of the asset nor transferred control of the              資 產 的 程 度 確 認 轉 讓 資 產。於 該 情 況
           asset, the Group continues to recognise the                      下，本集團亦確認一項關連負債。已轉
           transferred asset to the extent of the Group’s                   讓資產及關連負債乃按反映本集團已保
           continuing involvement. In that case, the Group also             留權利及責任的基準計量。
           recognises an associated liability. The transferred asset
           and the associated liability are measured on a basis

           has retained.

           Continuing involvement that takes the form of a                  持續涉及指本集團就已轉讓資產作出的

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT                                       2.4 主要會計政策概要（續）
    ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)
   Impairment of financial assets                                  金融資產減值
   The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit           本集團確認對並非按公允價值計入損益
   losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair       的所有債務工具預期信貸虧損（「預 期
   value through profit or loss. ECLs are based on the             信貸虧損」）的撥備。預期信貸虧損乃基
   difference between the contractual cash flows due in            於根據合約到期的合約現金流量與本集
   accordance with the contract and all the cash flows             團預期收取的所有現金流量之間的差額
   that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an             而 釐 定，並 以 原 實 際 利 率 的 近 似 值 貼
   approximation of the original effective interest rate. The      現。預期現金流量將包括出售所持抵押
   expected cash flows will include cash flows from the            的現金流量或組成合約條款的其他信貸
   sale of collateral held or other credit enhancements            提升措施。
   that are integral to the contractual terms.

   General approach                                                一般方法
   ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit                  預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自首
   exposures for which there has not been a significant           次確認起未有顯著增加的信貸風險而
   increase in credit risk since initial recognition, ECLs are    言，預 期 信 貸 虧 損 為 未 來12個 月 內 可
   provided for credit losses that result from default            能發生違約事件而導致的信貸虧損（12
   events that are possible within the next 12 months (a          個月預期信貸虧損）計提撥備。就自初
   12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which            始確認起經已顯著增加的信貸風險而
   there has been a significant increase in credit risk           言，不論何時發生違約，於餘下風險年
   since initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for    期內的預期信貸虧損均須計提虧損撥備
   credit losses expected over the remaining life of the          （全期預期信貸虧損）。
   exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a
   lifetime ECL).

   At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether              於各報告日期，本集團評估財務工具的
   the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased         信貸風險自首次確認以來是否大幅增
   significantly since initial recognition. When making the        加。進行評估時，本集團會比較報告日
   assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default            期就財務工具產生的違約風險與於首次
   occurring on the financial instrument as at the                 確認日期就財務工具產生的違約風險，
   reporting date with the risk of a default occurring on          並會考慮合理及有理據且毋須花費過度
   the financial instrument as at the date of initial              成本或精力即可獲得的資料，包括過往
   recognition and considers reasonable and supportable            經驗及前瞻性資料。
   information that is available without undue cost or
   effort, including historical and forward-looking
   information.

   deferred tax asset to be utilised. Unrecognised                  遞延稅項資產乃按可能獲得足夠應課稅
   deferred tax assets are reassessed at the end of each            盈利以致可收回全部或部分遞延稅項資

3. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                     3. 主要會計判斷及估計（續）
   JUDGEMENTS AND ESTIMATES
  (continued)

  Estimation uncertainty (continued)                            估計不明朗因素（續）
  Estimated useful lives and impairment of                      舉辦權及服務合約的估計可使用年期及
  sponsorship rights and service contracts                      減值
  The Group’s management estimates the useful lives of          本集團管理層估計醫院舉辦權及服務合
  the sponsorship rights and service contracts of the           約之可用年期為不確定。此估計基於所
  hospitals are indefinite. This estimate is based on an        有相關因素分析作出，如類似性質及功
  analysis of all of the relevant factors, such as the          能預期使用年期、權利控制權期限及權
  expected useful life of similar nature and functions, the     利使用之法律或類似限制（包括透過舉
  period of control over the right and legal or similar         辦權向舉辦權醫院執行委員會委任代表
  limits on the use of the rights (including the ability to     以重續屆滿服務合約的能力），使用年
  renew the service contracts upon their maturity               期取決於醫院運營年限。基於此分析，
  through the sponsorship rights to appoint                     本集團管理層認為權利預期為本集團產
  representatives in the board of trustees of the               生淨現金流入之期限無可預見限制。
  Sponsored Hospitals), and the useful life is dependent
  on the operation periods of the hospitals. Based on
  this analysis, the Group’s management considers there
  is no foreseeable limit to the period over which the
  right is expected to generate net cash inflows for the
  Group.

  The carrying amounts of the sponsorship rights and            舉辦權及服務合約之賬面值每年檢討，
  service contracts are reviewed to assess whether their        以評估其可收回金額是否減至低於賬面
  recoverable amounts have declined below their carrying        值。可收回金額基於獲分配舉辦權及服
  amounts annually. The recoverable amount is                   務合約之現金產生單位（以使用價值與
  determined based on the cash-generating unit to which         公允價值減出售成本間之較高者為準）
  the sponsorship rights and service contracts have been        釐定。減值計算要求本集團估計預期從
  allocated, which is the higher of the value in use and        現金產生單位產生之未來現金流量（納
  fair value less costs of disposals. The impairment            入服務合約預期未來現金流估計）及合
  calculation requires the Group to estimate the future         適貼現率，以計算當前價值。倘可收回
  cash flows expected to arise from the cash-generating         金額低於其賬面值，確認減值虧損以將
  unit, which has taken into account the estimation of          資產調低至其可收回金額。
  expected future cash flows of service contracts, and a
  suitable discount rate in order to calculate the present
  value. Where the recoverable amount is less than its
  carrying amount, an impairment loss is recognised to
  write the assets down to its recoverable amount.

  As at 31 December 2019, the carrying amount of the           於2019年12月31日，舉辦權及服務合
  sponsorship rights and service contracts was                 約之賬面值為人民幣1,844,203,000元
  RMB1,844,203,000 (2018: RMB1,728,026,000). Further           （2018年︰人 民 幣1,728,026,000元），

                 Hospitals;

                 complete satisfaction of that performance
                 obligation. The progress towards the complete
                 satisfaction of performance obligation is

                 individual products or services transferred by the

26. TRADE RECEIVABLES (continued)                               26. 應收貿易款項（續）
   An impairment analysis is performed at each reporting                  減值分析在每個報告日使用準備矩陣計
   date using a provision matrix to measure expected                      量預期信貸虧損。撥備率基於具有相似
   credit losses. The provision rates are based on days                   虧損模式的各個客戶類別分組逾期天
   past due for groupings of various customer segments                    數。該計算反映了概率加權結果、貨幣
   with similar loss patterns. The calculation reflects the               時值以及在報告日期可獲得的有關過去
   probability-weighted outcome, the time value of money                  事件、目前狀況和對未來經濟狀況的預
   and reasonable and supportable information that is                     測的合理和有理據的資料。一般而言，
   available at the reporting date about past events,                     如果應收貿易款項逾期三年以上，並且
   current conditions and forecasts of future economic                    並無執行行動，則撇銷款項。
   conditions. Generally, trade receivables are written off
   if past due for more than three years and are not
   subject to enforcement activity.

   Set out below is the information about the credit risk                 有關本集團的應收貿易款項的信貸風險
   exposure on the Group’s trade receivables using a                      資料載列如下：

39. RESERVES                                                   39. 儲備
   The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the                   本集團於本年度和以前年度的儲備及其
   movements therein for the current and prior years are         變動已於財務報表第110頁至第111頁
   presented in the consolidated statement of changes in         綜合權益變動報表中列示。
   equity on pages 110 to 111 of the financial
   statements.

   (a) Statutory surplus reserve                                 (a) 法定盈餘公積
        Pursuant to the relevant laws and regulations in            根據中國內地的有關法律法規，
        Mainland China, a portion of the profits of the             本公司在中國內地的子公司的部
        Company’s subsidiaries in Mainland China has                分利潤已轉入法定盈餘公積，使
        been transferred to the statutory surplus reserve           用受限。
        which are restricted to use.

   (b) Share-based payment reserve                               (b) 股份付款儲備
        The share-based payment reserve comprises the               以股份為基礎的付款儲備包括尚
        fair value of shares awarded which are yet to be            未歸屬的獎勵股份公允價值，誠
        vested, as further explained in the accounting              如財務報表附註2.4中以股份為基
        policy for share-based payments in note 2.4 to              礎的付款會計政策中進一步解
        the financial statements. The amount will either            釋。該款項將在歸屬相關股份時
        be transferred to treasury share reserve and                轉移至庫存股份儲備及資本儲
        capital reserve when the related share awards are           備，或在相關股份獎勵屆滿或沒
        vested, or be transferred to retained profits               收時轉移至保留溢利。
        should the related share awards expire or be
        forfeited.

   (c) Actuarial reserve                                         (c) 精算儲備
        The actuarial changes reserve comprises the                 精算變動儲備包括了由於人口假
        remeasurements arising from changes in                      設、財務假設和經驗調整的變化
        demographic assumptions, financial assumptions              而產生的重新計量，誠如在財務
        and experiences adjustments, as further explained           報表附註2.4中界定福利計劃的會
        in the accounting policy for defined benefit plan in        計政策中進一步解釋。
        note 2.4 to the financial statements.

45. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE                                    45. 金融工具的公允價值及公允
    HIERARCHY OF FINANCIAL                                           價值層級
    INSTRUMENTS
   Management has assessed that the fair values of cash            管理層評估了現金及現金等價物、存款
   and cash equivalents, certificate of deposit, trade             證、應收貿易款項、計入預付款、保證
   receivables, financial assets included in prepayments,          金及其他應收款項的金融資產、與關聯
   deposits and other receivables, balances with related           方的結餘、應付貿易款項、計入其他應
   parties, trade payables, financial liabilities included in      付款項及應計費用的金融負債、應付舉
   other payables and accruals, payables to the                    辦權醫院款項以及計息銀行借款，由於
   Sponsored Hospitals and interest-bearing bank                   這些工具到期期限較短，所以其公允價
   borrowings approximate to their carrying amounts                值接近賬面價值。
   largely due to the short term maturities of these
   instruments.

   The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are    金融資產及負債的公允價值按當前交易
   included at the amount at which the instrument could           （強制或清算出售除外）中雙方自 願 交
   be exchanged in a current transaction between willing          換工具的金額入賬。以下方法和假設被

   discounting the expected future cash flows using rates          期未來現金流量，採用具有類似條款、
   currently available for instruments with similar terms,         信貸風險及剩餘到期期限的工具現時可
   credit risk and remaining maturities.                           得利率貼現來計算。

   The fair value of the listed financial asset at FVTPL are       以公允價值計量且其變動計入損益的上
   based on quoted market prices.                                  市金融資產的公允價值基於市場報價。

   The fair value of the unlisted financial assets at FVTPL        以公允價值計量且其變動計入損益的非
   are estimated by using a discounted cash flow                   上市金融資產的公允價值是根據具有類

    OBJECTIVES AND POLICIES
  The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise             本集團的主要金融工具包括現金和現金
  cash and cash equivalents, certificate of deposit,               等價物、存款證、以公允價值計量且其
  financial assets at FVTPL, payables to the Sponsored             變動計入損益的金融資產、應付舉辦權
  Hospitals and interest-bearing bank borrowings. The              醫院的款項以及計息銀行借款。這些金
  main purpose of these financial instruments is to                融工具的主要目的是為集團的運營提供
  finance the Group’s operations. The Group has various            資金。本集團還有其他各種金融資產和
  other financial assets and liabilities such as receivables       負債，例如來自IOT醫院的應收款項、
  from IOT hospitals, loan to a sponsored hospital, trade          提供給舉辦權醫院的貸款、應收貿易款
  receivables, balances with related parties, financial            項、與關聯方的結餘，計入預付款、保
  assets included in prepayments, deposits and other               證金和其他應收款項中的金融資產、應
  receivables, trade payables, financial liabilities included      付貿易款項、計入其他應付款項及應計
  in other payables and accruals, and lease liabilities,           費用的金融負債，及租賃負債，該等主
  which mainly arise directly from its operations.                 要直接來自其經營活動。

  The main risks arising from the Group’s financial                本集團金融工具產生的主要風險是利率
  instruments are interest rate risk, foreign currency risk,       風險、外幣風險、信貸風險、流動性風
  credit risk, liquidity risk and equity price risk. The board     險及權益價格風險。董事會審查並同意
  of directors reviews and agrees policies for managing            管理每種風險的政策，現概述如下。
  each of these risks and they are summarised below.

  Interest rate risk                                               利率風險
  The Group’s exposure to the risk of changes in market            本集團承受的市場利率變動風險主要與
  interest rates relates primarily to the Group’s loan to a        本集團向舉辦權醫院提供的貸款以及具
  sponsored hospital and interest-bearing bank                     有浮動利率的計息銀行借款有關。本集
  borrowings with floating interest rates. The Group does          團並無使用衍生金融工具對沖其利率風
  not use derivative financial instruments to hedge its            險。本集團通過密切監控利率變動並定
  interest rate risk. The Group mitigates this risk by             期審查其可用信貸額度及其使用情況來
  closely monitoring the movements in interest rates and           減輕這種風險。
  reviewing its available credit facilities and their
  utilisation regularly.

           Liquidity risk                                                        流動資金風險
           Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group will encounter              流動資金風險指本集團因資金短缺而難
           difficulty in meeting financial obligations due to                    以履行財務責任的風險。本集團面臨的
           shortage of funds. The Group’s exposure to liquidity                  流動資金風險主要來自金融資產和負債
           risk arises primarily from mismatches of the maturities               的到期日錯配。本集團通過考慮金融負
           of financial assets and liabilities. The Group monitors               債和金融資產的到期日來監控資金短缺
           its risk to a shortage of funds by considering the                    的風險。
           maturities of both its financial liabilities and financial
           assets.

           The Group’s objective is to maintain a balance                        本集團的目標是通過使用銀行借款在資
           between continuity of funding and flexibility through                 金的連續性和靈活性之間保持平衡。本
           the use of bank borrowings. The Group aims to                         集團旨在維持足夠的現金及現金等價
           maintain sufficient cash and cash equivalents to meet                 物，以滿足其流動資金需求。
           its liquidity requirements.

   As further explained in note 2.2 to the financial            如財務報表附註2.2進一步解釋，本集
   statements, the Group adopted IFRS 16 on 1 January           團於2019年1月1日採用經修訂的追溯
   2019 using the modified retrospective approach. Under        法採用了國際財務報告準則第16號。
   this approach, the comparative amounts in the financial      在這種方法下，財務報表中的可資比較
   statements were not restated and continued to be             金額沒有重列，而是根據以前的準則，
   reported under the requirements of the previous              國際會計準則第17號和相關詮釋要求
   standard, IAS 17, and related interpretations.               繼續進行報告。
